### Folio 15 (`Page ID: 2209-15` | `175` Words | `17` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> بِحَفِيظٍ﴾ ١، ﴿وَأَعْرِضْ عَنِ الْمُشْرِكِينَ﴾ ٢، ﴿وَمَا جَعَلْنَاكَ عَلَيْهِمْ حَفِيظاً وَمَا "٩٢أ" أَنْتَ عَلَيْهِمْ بِوَكِيلٍ﴾ ٣، ﴿وَلا تَسُبُّوا الَّذِينَ يَدْعُونَ مِنْ دُونِ اللَّهِ فَيَسُبُّوا اللَّهَ عَدْواً بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ﴾ ٤، ﴿فَذَرْهُمْ٥ وَمَا يَفْتَرُونَ﴾ ٦، ﴿قُلْ يَا قَوْمِ اعْمَلُوا عَلَى مَكَانَتِكُمْ﴾ ٧، ﴿قُلِ انْتَظِرُوا إِنَّا مُنْتَظِرُونَ﴾ ٨، ﴿لَسْتَ مِنْهُمْ فِي شَيْءٍ إِنَّمَا أَمْرُهُمْ إِلَى اللَّهِ﴾ ٩"ن": آية السيف"م": ﴿وَذَرِ الَّذِينَ اتَّخَذُوا دِينَهُمْ لَعِباً وَلَهْواً﴾ ١٠"ن": آية القتال"م": ﴿قُلْ إِنِّي أَخَافُ إِنْ عَصَيْتُ رَبِّي عَذَابَ يَوْمٍ عَظِيمٍ﴾ ١١"ن": في الفتح ﴿لِيَغْفِرَ لَكَ اللَّهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِكَ وَمَا تَأَخَّرَ﴾ ١٢"م": ﴿وَلا تَأْكُلُوا مِمَّا لَمْ يُذْكَرِ اسْمُ اللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنَّهُ لَفِسْقٌ﴾ ١٣"ن": في المائدة ﴿الْيَوْمَ أُحِلَّ لَكُمُ الطَّيِّبَاتُ وَطَعَامُ الَّذِينَ أُوتُوا الْكِتَابَ حِلٌّ لَكُمْ١٤﴾ ومعنى الطيبات الذبائح سورة الأعراف "م": موضعان"ن": موضعان.
١ آية ١٠٤.٢ آية ١٠٦..٣ آية ١٠٧.٤ آية ١٠٨.٥ في الأصل: وذرهم. وما أثبتناه من المصحف الشريف.٦ الآيتان ١١٢ و ١٣٧.٧ آية ١٣٥.٨ آية ١٥٨.٩ آية ١٥٩.١٠ آية ٧٠.١١ آية ١١٥.١٢ الفتح ٢.١٣ ١٢١.١٤ المائدة ٥.

**English**:
> Guardian, keeper 1, ﴿Follow, O Messenger, what has been revealed to you from your Sovereign Lord - there is no deity except Him - and turn away from those who associate others with Allāh.﴾ 2, ﴿But if Allāh had willed, they would not have associated. And We have not appointed you over them as a guardian, nor are you a manager over them.﴾ 3, ﴿And do not insult those they invoke other than Allāh, lest they insult Allāh in enmity without knowledge. Thus We have made pleasing to every community their deeds. Then to their Sovereign Lord is their return, and He will inform them about what they used to do.﴾ 4, ﴿And thus We have made for every prophet an enemy - devils from mankind and jinn, inspiring to one another decorative speech in delusion. But if your Sovereign Lord had willed, they would not have done it, so leave them and that which they invent.﴾ 6, say O a people, folk do, work, act upon your working 7, ﴿Do they then wait for anything except that the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah) should come to them or your Sovereign Lord should come or that there come some of the signs of your Sovereign Lord? The Day that some of the signs of your Sovereign Lord will come no soul will benefit from its faith as long as it had not believed before or had earned through its faith some good. Say, "Wait. Indeed, we also are waiting."﴾ 8, ﴿Indeed, those who have divided their religion and become sects - you, O Messenger, are not associated with them in anything. Their affair is only left to Allāh; then He will inform them about what they used to do.﴾ 9 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿And leave those who take their religion as amusement and diversion and whom the worldly life has deluded. But remind with the Qur'an, lest a soul be given up to destruction for what it earned; it will have other than Allāh no protector and no intercessor. And if it should offer every compensation, it would not be taken from it. Those are the ones who are given to destruction for what they have earned. For them will be a drink of scalding water and an painful punishment because they used to disbelieve.﴾ 10 actually: a verse (āyah) fighting, battle M: ﴿Say, "Indeed I fear, if I should disobey my Sovereign Lord, the punishment of a tremendous Day."﴾ 11 actually: in al-Fatḥ ﴿That Allāh may forgive for you what preceded of your sin and what will follow and complete His favor upon you and guide you to a Straight Path﴾ 12 M: ﴿And do not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has not been mentioned, for indeed, it is grave disobedience. And indeed do the devils (Ash-Shayāṭīn) inspire their allies among men to dispute with you. And if you were to obey them, indeed, you would be associators of others with Him.﴾ 13 actually: in al-Māʾidah ﴿This day All good foods have been made lawful, and the food of those who were given the Inscribed Scripture is lawful for you and your food is lawful for them. And lawful in marriage are chaste women from among those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn) and chaste women from among those who were given the Inscribed Scripture before you, when you have given them their due compensation, desiring chastity, not unlawful sexual intercourse or taking secret lovers. - **[S1] AR**: بِحَفِيظٍ﴾ ١، ﴿وَأَعْرِضْ عَنِ الْمُشْرِكِينَ﴾ ٢، ﴿وَمَا جَعَلْنَاكَ عَلَيْهِمْ حَفِيظاً وَمَا "٩٢أ" أَنْتَ عَلَيْهِمْ بِوَكِيلٍ﴾ ٣، ﴿وَلا تَسُبُّوا الَّذِينَ يَدْعُونَ مِنْ دُونِ اللَّهِ فَيَسُبُّوا اللَّهَ عَدْواً بِغَيْرِ عِلْمٍ﴾ ٤، ﴿فَذَرْهُمْ٥ وَمَا يَفْتَرُونَ﴾ ٦، ﴿قُلْ يَا قَوْمِ اعْمَلُوا عَلَى مَكَانَتِكُمْ﴾ ٧، ﴿قُلِ انْتَظِرُوا إِنَّا مُنْتَظِرُونَ﴾ ٨، ﴿لَسْتَ مِنْهُمْ فِي شَيْءٍ إِنَّمَا أَمْرُهُمْ إِلَى اللَّهِ﴾ ٩ "ن": آية السيف "م": ﴿وَذَرِ الَّذِينَ اتَّخَذُوا دِينَهُمْ لَعِباً وَلَهْواً﴾ ١٠ "ن": آية القتال "م": ﴿قُلْ إِنِّي أَخَافُ إِنْ عَصَيْتُ رَبِّي عَذَابَ يَوْمٍ عَظِيمٍ﴾ ١١ "ن": في الفتح ﴿لِيَغْفِرَ لَكَ اللَّهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِكَ وَمَا تَأَخَّرَ﴾ ١٢ "م": ﴿وَلا تَأْكُلُوا مِمَّا لَمْ يُذْكَرِ اسْمُ اللَّهِ عَلَيْهِ وَإِنَّهُ لَفِسْقٌ﴾ ١٣ "ن": في المائدة ﴿الْيَوْمَ أُحِلَّ لَكُمُ الطَّيِّبَاتُ وَطَعَامُ الَّذِينَ أُوتُوا الْكِتَابَ حِلٌّ لَكُمْ١٤﴾ ومعنى
  **[S1] EN**: Guardian, keeper 1, ﴿Follow, O Messenger, what has been revealed to you from your Sovereign Lord - there is no deity except Him - and turn away from those who associate others with Allāh.﴾ 2, ﴿But if Allāh had willed, they would not have associated. And We have not appointed you over them as a guardian, nor are you a manager over them.﴾ 3, ﴿And do not insult those they invoke other than Allāh, lest they insult Allāh in enmity without knowledge. Thus We have made pleasing to every community their deeds. Then to their Sovereign Lord is their return, and He will inform them about what they used to do.﴾ 4, ﴿And thus We have made for every prophet an enemy - devils from mankind and jinn, inspiring to one another decorative speech in delusion. But if your Sovereign Lord had willed, they would not have done it, so leave them and that which they invent.﴾ 6, say O a people, folk do, work, act upon your working 7, ﴿Do they then wait for anything except that the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah) should come to them or your Sovereign Lord should come or that there come some of the signs of your Sovereign Lord? The Day that some of the signs of your Sovereign Lord will come no soul will benefit from its faith as long as it had not believed before or had earned through its faith some good. Say, "Wait. Indeed, we also are waiting."﴾ 8, ﴿Indeed, those who have divided their religion and become sects - you, O Messenger, are not associated with them in anything. Their affair is only left to Allāh; then He will inform them about what they used to do.﴾ 9 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿And leave those who take their religion as amusement and diversion and whom the worldly life has deluded. But remind with the Qur'an, lest a soul be given up to destruction for what it earned; it will have other than Allāh no protector and no intercessor. And if it should offer every compensation, it would not be taken from it. Those are the ones who are given to destruction for what they have earned. For them will be a drink of scalding water and an painful punishment because they used to disbelieve.﴾ 10 actually: a verse (āyah) fighting, battle M: ﴿Say, "Indeed I fear, if I should disobey my Sovereign Lord, the punishment of a tremendous Day."﴾ 11 actually: in al-Fatḥ ﴿That Allāh may forgive for you what preceded of your sin and what will follow and complete His favor upon you and guide you to a Straight Path﴾ 12 M: ﴿And do not eat of that upon which the name of Allāh has not been mentioned, for indeed, it is grave disobedience. And indeed do the devils (Ash-Shayāṭīn) inspire their allies among men to dispute with you. And if you were to obey them, indeed, you would be associators of others with Him.﴾ 13 actually: in al-Māʾidah ﴿This day All good foods have been made lawful, and the food of those who were given the Inscribed Scripture is lawful for you and your food is lawful for them. And lawful in marriage are chaste women from among those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn) and chaste women from among those who were given the Inscribed Scripture before you, when you have given them their due compensation, desiring chastity, not unlawful sexual intercourse or taking secret lovers. And whoever denies the faith - his work has become worthless, and he, in the Hereafter, will be among the losers.﴾ and meaning (maʿnā)

**English**:
> M: enjoyment to 1, ﴿Take what is given freely, enjoin what is good, and turn away from the ignorant.﴾ 2 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) addition L seven actually: six M: ﴿And if they incline to peace, then incline to it also and rely upon Allāh. Indeed, it is He who is the Hearing, the Knowing.﴾ 3 actually: a verse (āyah) fighting, battle M: ﴿O you who have believed, when you meet those who stubbornly reject established reality (Al-Kāfirūn) advancing for battle, do not turn to them your backs in flight.﴾ 4 actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by it (baʿdahu) and he ﴿And whoever turns his back to them on such a day, unless swerving as a strategy for war or joining another company, has certainly returned with anger upon him from Allāh, and his refuge is the Hellfire of retributive consequence (Jahannam) - and wretched is the destination.﴾ 5 M: ﴿They ask you, O Messenger, about the bounties of war. Say, "The decision concerning bounties is for Allāh and the Messenger." So fear Allāh and amend that which is between you and obey Allāh and His Messenger, if you should be believers.﴾ 6 actually: ﴿And know that anything you obtain of war booty - then indeed, for Allāh is ever one fifth of it and for the Messenger and for his near relatives and the orphans, the needy, and the stranded traveler, if you have believed in Allāh and in that which We sent down to Our Servant on the day of criterion - the day when the two armies met. And Allāh, over all things, is competent.﴾ 7 And in gather ﴿And what Allāh restored to His Messenger from the people of the towns - it is for Allāh and for the Messenger and for his near relatives and orphans and the stranded traveler - so that it will not be a perpetual distribution among the rich from among you. And whatever the Messenger has given you - take; and what he has forbidden you - refrain from. And fear Allāh; indeed, Allāh is ever severe in penalty.﴾ 8 M: ﴿But Allāh would not punish them while you, O Messenger, are among them, and Allāh would not punish them while they seek forgiveness.﴾ 9 actually: ﴿But why should Allāh not punish them while they obstruct people from al-Masjid al- Haram and they were not fit to be its guardians? Its true guardians are not but the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār), but most of them do not know.﴾ 10 M: ﴿Say to those who have disbelieved that if they cease, what has previously occurred will be forgiven for them. But if they return to hostility - then the precedent of the former rebellious peoples has already taken place.﴾ 11 actually: some, part of the legal ruling on her here And in al-Baqarah ﴿Fight them until there is no more fitnah and until worship is acknowledged to be for Allāh. But if they cease, then there is to be no aggression except against the oppressors.﴾ 12 any Indeed did not desist, stop, refrain 1 a verse (āyah) 183. 2 a verse (āyah) 199. 3 a verse (āyah) 16. 4 a verse (āyah) 15. 5 a verse (āyah) 16. 6 a verse (āyah) 1. 7 a verse (āyah) 41. 8 gather 7. 9 a verse (āyah) 33. 10 a verse (āyah) 34. 11 a verse (āyah) 38. And in the foundational principle: Year (sanah) / Sunnah. and what his Athbatn he orthography script (rasm) the Qur'ānic Codex (al-Muṣḥaf) the exalted in. and investigates: Ghīḍāḥ Pausing in Recitation (al-Waqf) and Resuming (al-Ibtidāʾ) 283 and the contentment 78. 12 al-Baqarah 193.

> "م": ﴿إنْ يَكُنْ مِنْكُمْ عِشْرُونَ صَابِرُونَ يَغْلِبُوا مِائَتَيْنِ﴾ ١"ن": ﴿الْآنَ خَفَّفَ اللَّهُ عَنْكُمْ﴾ ٢"م": ﴿وَالَّذِينَ آمَنُوا وَلَمْ يُهَاجِرُوا مَا لَكُمْ مِنْ وَلَايَتِهِمْ مِنْ شَيْءٍ حَتَّى يُهَاجِرُوا﴾ ٣وكانوا يتوارثون بالهجرة دون النسب"ن": ﴿وَأُولُوا الْأَرْحَامِ بَعْضُهُمْ أَوْلَى بِبَعْضٍ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ﴾ ٤ سورة التوبة "م": سبعة"ن": أربعة عشر"م": ﴿وَبَشِّرِ٥ الَّذِينَ كَفَرُوا بِعَذَابٍ أَلِيمٍ﴾ ٦"ن": ﴿إِلَّا الَّذِينَ عَاهَدْتُمْ مِنَ الْمُشْرِكِينَ﴾ ٧ على قول من فسر العذاب بالقتل"م": ﴿كَيْفَ يَكُونُ لِلْمُشْرِكِينَ عَهْدٌ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ وَعِنْدَ رَسُولِهِ﴾ ٨ الآية"ن": لبعض حكمها الاستثناء بعده وهو ﴿إِلَّا الَّذِينَ عَاهَدْتُمْ عِنْدَ الْمَسْجِدِ الْحَرَامِ﴾ ٩"م": ﴿وَالَّذِينَ يَكْنِزُونَ الذَّهَبَ وَالْفِضَّةَ﴾ ١٠"ن": لما فضل من المال ﴿إِنَّمَا الصَّدَقَاتُ "٩٢ب" لِلْفُقَرَاءِ﴾ ١١ و ﴿خُذْ مِنْ أَمْوَالِهِمْ صَدَقَةً﴾ ١٢ أي الزكاة الواجبة"م": ﴿إِلاّ تَنْفِرُوا يُعَذِّبْكُمْ عَذَاباً أَلِيماً﴾ ١٣ و ﴿انْفِرُوا خِفَافاً وَثِقَالاً﴾ ١٤

**English**:
> M: ﴿O Prophet, urge those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn) to battle. If there are among you twenty who are steadfast, they will overcome two hundred. And if there are among you one hundred who are steadfast, they will overcome a thousand of those who have disbelieved because they are a people who do not understand.﴾ 1 actually: ﴿Now, Allāh has lightened the hardship for you, and He knows that among you is weakness. So if there are from you one hundred who are steadfast, they will overcome two hundred. And if there are among you a thousand, they will overcome two thousand by permission of Allāh. And Allāh is ever with the steadfast.﴾ 2 M: ﴿Indeed, those who have believed and emigrated and fought with their wealth and lives in the cause of Allāh and those who gave shelter and aided - they are allies of one another. But those who attained conviction in established truth and did not emigrate - for you there is no guardianship of them until they emigrate. And if they seek help of you for the religion, then you must help, except against a people between yourselves and whom is a treaty. And Allāh is ever Seeing of what you do.﴾ 3 be inherit in the emigrate without the lineage actually: ﴿And those who attained conviction in established truth after the initial emigration and emigrated and fought with you - they are of you. But those of blood relationship are more entitled to inheritance in the decree of Allāh. Indeed, Allāh is ever Knowing of all things.﴾ 4 Sūrah (Chapter of) repentance (al-Tawbah) M: seven actually: four ten M: ﴿And it is an announcement from Allāh and His Messenger to the people on the day of the greater the sacred Pilgrimage (Al-Ḥajj) that Allāh is ever disassociated from the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn), and so is His Messenger. So if you repent, that is best for you; but if you turn away - then know that you will not cause failure to Allāh. And give tidings to those who stubbornly reject established reality (Al-Kāfirūn) of an painful punishment.﴾ 6 actually: ﴿Excepted are those with whom you made a treaty among those who associate partners with Allāh (Al-Mushrikūn) and then they have not been deficient toward you in anything or supported anyone against you; so complete for them their treaty until their term has ended. Indeed, Allāh loves the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) who fear Him.﴾ 7 upon word, speech, saying from stream torment (al-ʿAdhāb) in the fight M: ﴿How can there be for those who associate partners with Allāh (Al-Mushrikūn) a treaty in the sight of Allāh and with His Messenger, except for those with whom you made a treaty at al-Masjid al-Haram? So as long as they are upright toward you, be upright toward them. Indeed, Allāh loves the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) who fear Him.﴾ 8 Verse (al-Āyah) actually: some, part of the legal ruling on her exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by it (baʿdahu) and he ﴿How can there be for those who associate partners with Allāh (Al-Mushrikūn) a treaty in the sight of Allāh and with His Messenger, except for those with whom you made a treaty at al-Masjid al-Haram? So as long as they are upright toward you, be upright toward them. Indeed, Allāh loves the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) who fear Him.﴾ 9 M: ﴿O you who have believed, indeed many of the scholars and the monks devour the wealth of people unjustly and avert them from the way of Allāh. And those who hoard gold and silver and spend it not in the way of Allāh - give them tidings of an painful punishment.﴾ 10 actually: for what The Virtue (Faḍl) of from wealth, property Truly charities 9a2b poor 11 and ﴿Take, O, Muhammad, from their wealth a charity by which you purify them and cause them increase, and invoke Allāh 's blessings upon them. Indeed, your invocations are reassurance for them. And Allāh is ever Hearing and Knowing.﴾ 12 any the Alms-tax (al-Zakāh) the obligatory (wājibah) M: ﴿If you do not go forth, He will punish you with an painful punishment and will replace you with another people, and you will not harm Him at all. And Allāh is ever holds omnipotent power over all things.﴾ 13 and ﴿Go forth, whether light or heavy, and strive with your wealth and your lives in the cause of Allāh. That is better for you, if you only knew.﴾ 14 1 a verse (āyah) 65. 2 a verse (āyah) 191. 3 a verse (āyah) 72. 4 a verse (āyah) 75. 5 in the foundational principle: give tidings. and what his Athbatn from the Qur'ānic Codex (al-Muṣḥaf) the exalted in. 6 a verse (āyah) 3. 7 a verse (āyah) 4. 8 a verse (āyah) 7. 9 a verse (āyah) 7. 10 a verse (āyah) 34. 11 a verse (āyah) 60. 12 a verse (āyah) 103. 13 a verse (āyah) 39. 14 a verse (āyah) 41.

### Folio 18 (`Page ID: 2209-18` | `160` Words | `15` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> "ن": ﴿وَمَا كَانَ الْمُؤْمِنُونَ لِيَنْفِرُوا كَافَّةً﴾ ١"م": ﴿عَفَا اللَّهُ عَنْكَ لِمَ أَذِنْتَ لَهُمْ﴾ ٢"ن": في النور ﴿فَإِذَا اسْتَأْذَنُوكَ لِبَعْضِ شَأْنِهِمْ فَأْذَنْ لِمَنْ شِئْتَ مِنْهُمْ﴾ ٣"م": ﴿الْأَعْرَابُ أَشَدُّ كُفْراً وَنِفَاقاً إلى عَلَيْهِمْ﴾ ٤ الآيتان"ن": لبعض حكمها الآية التي بعدها ﴿وَمِنَ الْأَعْرَابِ مَنْ يُؤْمِنُ بِاللَّهِ وَالْيَوْمِ الْآخِرِ﴾ ٥ سورة يونس  ﵇"م": سبعة"م": ﴿فَانْتَظِرُوا إِنِّي مَعَكُمْ مِنَ الْمُنْتَظِرِينَ﴾ ٦، ﴿وَإِنْ كَذَّبُوكَ فَقُلْ لِي عَمَلِي وَلَكُمْ عَمَلُكُمْ﴾ ٧، ﴿وَإِمَّا نُرِيَنَّكَ بَعْضَ الَّذِي نَعِدُهُمْ أَوْ نَتَوَفَّيَنَّكَ﴾ ٨، ﴿أَفَأَنْتَ تُكْرِهُ النَّاسَ حَتَّى يَكُونُوا مُؤْمِنِينَ﴾ ٩، ﴿فَهَلْ يَنْتَظِرُونَ إِلَّا مِثْلَ أَيَّامِ الَّذِينَ خَلَوْا مِنْ قَبْلِهِمْ﴾ ١٠، ﴿فَمَنِ اهْتَدَى فَإِنَّمَا يَهْتَدِي لِنَفْسِهِ﴾ ١١، ﴿وَاصْبِرْ حَتَّى يَحْكُمَ اللَّهُ﴾ .١٢"ن": آية السيف سورة هود  ﵇"م": أربعة"م": ﴿إِنَّمَا أَنْتَ نَذِيرٌ﴾ . ١٣
١ آية ١٢٢.٢ آية ٤٣.٣ النور ٦٢.٤ الآيتان ٩٧ و ٩٨.٥ آية ٩٩.٦ آية ٢٠.٧ آية ٤١.٨ آية ٤٦.٩ آية ٩٩.١٠ آية ١٠٢.١١ آية ١٠٨.١٢ آية ١٠٩.١٣ آية ١٢.

**English**:
> Actually: ﴿And it is not for those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn) to go forth to battle all at once. For there should separate from every division of them a group remaining to obtain understanding in the religion and warn their people when they return to them that they might be cautious.﴾ 1a"m: ﴿May Allāh pardon you, O Messenger; why did you give them permission to remain behind? You should not have until it was evident to you who were truthful and you knew who were the liars.﴾ 2 actually: in the light when your Astaʾdhanū some, part of status them announcer to whoever will, wish from them 3 M: ﴿The bedouins are stronger in disbelief and hypocrisy and more likely not to know the limits of what laws Allāh has revealed to His Messenger. And Allāh is ever Knowing and Wise.﴾ 4 the two Verses (al-Āyatān) actually: some, part of the legal ruling on her Verse (al-Āyah) which followed by them (baʿdahā) ﴿But among the bedouins are some who believe in Allāh and the Last Day of Accountability and consider what they spend as means of nearness to Allāh and of obtaining invocations of the Messenger. Unquestionably, it is a means of nearness for them. Allāh shall admit them to His mercy. Indeed, Allāh is ever Forgiving and Merciful.﴾ 5 Sūrah (Chapter of) Yūnus ﵇ M: seven M: ﴿Hud said, "Already have defilement and anger fallen upon you from your Sovereign Lord. Do you dispute with me concerning mere names you have named them, you and your fathers, for which Allāh has not sent down any authority? Then wait; indeed, I am with you among those who wait."﴾ 6, ﴿And if they deny you, O Messenger, then say, "For me are my deeds, and for you are your deeds. You are disassociated from what I do, and I am disassociated from what you do."﴾ 7, ﴿And whether We show you some of what We promise them, O Messenger, or We take you in death, to Us is their return; then, either way, Allāh is ever a witness concerning what they are doing﴾ 8, ﴿And had your Sovereign Lord willed, those on earth would have believed - all of them entirely. Then, O Messenger, would you compel the people in order that they become believers?﴾ 9, ﴿So do they wait except for like what occurred in the days of those who passed on before them? Say, "Then wait; indeed, I am with you among those who wait."﴾ 10, ﴿Say, "O mankind, the truth has come to you from your Sovereign Lord, so whoever is guided is only guided for the benefit of his soul, and whoever goes astray only goes astray in violation against it. And I am not over you a manager."﴾ 11, ﴿And follow what is revealed to you, O Messenger, and be patient until Allāh shall judge. And He is the best of judges.﴾ 12 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) Prophet Hūd (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) ﵇ M: four M: ﴿Then would you possibly leave out some of what is revealed to you, or is your breast constrained by it because they say, "Why has there not been sent down to him a treasure or come with him an angel?" But you are only a warner. And Allāh is ever Disposer of all things.﴾. 13 1 a verse (āyah) 122. 2 a verse (āyah) 43. 3 the light 62. 4 the two Verses (al-Āyatān) 97 and 98. 5 a verse (āyah) 99. 6 a verse (āyah) 20. 7 a verse (āyah) 41. 8 a verse (āyah) 46. 9 a verse (āyah) 99. 10 a verse (āyah) 102. 11 a verse (āyah) 108. 12 a verse (āyah) 109. 13 a verse (āyah) 12.

**English**:
> M: ﴿And He gave you from all you asked of Him. And if you should count the favor of Allāh, you could not enumerate them. Indeed, mankind is generally most unjust and ungrateful.﴾ 1 actually: in al-Naḥl ﴿And if you should count the favors of Allāh, you could not enumerate them. Indeed, Allāh is ever Forgiving and Merciful.﴾ 2 say ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) stones M: five M: ﴿Let them eat and enjoy themselves and be diverted by false hope, for they are going to know.﴾ 3, ﴿And We have not created the heavens and the terrestrial earth and that between them except in truth. And indeed, the Hour is coming; so forgive with gracious forgiveness.﴾ 4, ﴿Do not extend your eyes toward that by which We have given enjoyment to certain categories of the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn), and do not grieve over them. And lower your wing to those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn)﴾ 6, ﴿Follow, O Messenger, what has been revealed to you from your Sovereign Lord - there is no deity except Him - and turn away from those who associate others with Allāh.﴾ 7 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿And say, "Indeed, I am the clear warner" -﴾ 8 actually: its judgment not for the Faẓh a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) al-Naḥl M: five actually: place M: ﴿But if they turn away, O Messenger - then only upon you is responsibility for clear notification.﴾ 9, ﴿Invite to the way of your Sovereign Lord with wisdom and good instruction, and argue with them in a way that is best. Indeed, your Sovereign Lord is most knowing of who has strayed from His way, and He is most knowing of who is rightly guided.﴾ 10, ﴿And be patient, O Messenger, and your patience is not but through Allāh. ١ آية ١٠٦.٢ آية ١٠٦.٣ آية ٦٧.٤ المائدة ٩٠.٥ هي الإسراء في المصحف.٦ آية ٥٤.٧ آية ٢٤.٨ التوبة ١١٣.٩ آية ١١٠.١٠ الأعراف ٢٠٥.١١ آية ٢٩.

**English**:
> M: ﴿Whoever disbelieves in Allāh after his belief... except for one who is forced to renounce his religion while his heart is secure in faith. But those who willingly open their breasts to disbelief, upon them is wrath from Allāh, and for them is a monumental and crushing penalty;﴾ 1 actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by it (baʿdahu) 93 Ūhū ﴿Whoever disbelieves in Allāh after his belief... except for one who is forced to renounce his religion while his heart is secure in faith. But those who willingly open their breasts to disbelief, upon them is wrath from Allāh, and for them is a monumental and crushing penalty;﴾ 2 M: ﴿And from the fruits of the palm trees and grapevines you take intoxicant and good provision. Indeed in that is a sign for a people who reason.﴾ 3 actually: in al-Māʾidah ﴿O you who have believed, indeed, intoxicants, gambling, sacrificing on stone alters to other than Allāh, and divining arrows are but defilement from the work of Satan (Ash-Shayṭān), so avoid it that you may be successful.﴾ 4 Sūrah (Chapter of) Glory be to 5 M: three actually: place M: ﴿your Sovereign Lord is most knowing of you. If He wills, He will have mercy upon you; or if He wills, He will punish you. And We have not sent you, O Messenger, over them as a manager.﴾ 6 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿And lower to them the wing of humility out of mercy and say, "my Sovereign Lord, have mercy upon them as they brought me up when I was small."﴾ 7 actually: some, part of the legal ruling on her in one who associates partners in free of blame ﴿It is not for the Prophet and those who have believed to ask forgiveness for those who associate partners with Allāh (Al-Mushrikūn), even if they were relatives, after it has become clear to them that they are companions of Hellfire.﴾ 8 M: ﴿Say, "Call upon Allāh or call upon the Most Merciful. Whichever name you call - to Him belong the best names." And do not recite too loudly in your the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) or too quietly but seek between that an intermediate way.﴾ 9 actually: in Al-Aʿrāf ﴿And remember your Sovereign Lord within yourself in humility and in fear without being apparent in speech - in the mornings and the evenings. And do not be among the heedless.﴾ 10 Sūrah (Chapter of) Al-Kahf M: place locus (mawḍiʿ) actually: five M: ﴿And say, "The truth is from your Sovereign Lord, so whoever wills - let him believe; and whoever wills - let him disbelieve." Indeed, We have prepared for the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn) a fire whose walls will surround them. And if they call for relief, they will be relieved with water like murky oil, which scalds their faces. Wretched is the drink, and evil is the resting place.﴾ 11 1 a verse (āyah) 106. 2 a verse (āyah) 106. 3 a verse (āyah) 67. 4 al-Māʾidah 90. 5 she al-Isrāʾ in the Qur'ānic Codex (al-Muṣḥaf). 6 a verse (āyah) 54. 7 a verse (āyah) 24. 8 repentance (al-Tawbah) 113. 9 a verse (āyah) 110. 10 Al-Aʿrāf 205. 11 a verse (āyah) 29.

> "ن": في التكوير ﴿وَمَا تَشَاءُونَ إِلَّا أَنْ يَشَاءَ اللَّهُ رَبُّ الْعَالَمِينَ﴾ ١وقيل محكمة سورة مريم  ﵍"م": خمسة"ن": موضعان"م": ﴿وَأَنْذِرْهُمْ يَوْمَ الْحَسْرَةِ﴾ ٢، ﴿فَلا تَعْجَلْ عَلَيْهِمْ﴾ ٣، ﴿قُلْ مَنْ كَانَ فِي الضَّلالَةِ فَلْيَمْدُدْ لَهُ الرَّحْمَنُ مَدّاً﴾ ٤"ن": آية السيف"م": ﴿فَخَلَفَ مِنْ بَعْدِهِمْ خَلْفٌ إلى غَيّاً﴾ ٥"ن": الاستثناء بعده وهو ﴿إِلَّا مَنْ تَابَ﴾ ٦"م": ﴿وَإِنْ مِنْكُمْ إِلَّا وَارِدُهَا﴾ ٧"ن": ﴿ثُمَّ نُنَجِّي الَّذِينَ اتَّقَوْا﴾ ٨ سورة طه "م": أربعة"ن": موضعان"م": ﴿فَاصْبِرْ٩ عَلَى مَا يَقُولُونَ﴾ ١٠، ﴿وَلا تَمُدَّنَ عَيْنَيْكَ إِلَى مَا مَتَّعْنَا بِهِ أَزْوَاجاً مِنْهُمْ زَهْرَةَ الْحَيَاةِ الدُّنْيَا﴾ ١١، ﴿قُلْ كُلٌّ مُتَرَبِّصٌ فَتَرَبَّصُوا﴾ .١٢
١ التكوير ٢٩.٢ آية ٣٩.٣ آية ٨٤.٤ آية ٧٥.٥ آية ٥٩.٦ آية ٦٠.٧ آية ٧١.٨ آية ٧٢.٩ في الأصل: واصبر. والصواب ما في المصحف الشريف.١٠ آية ١٣٠.١١ آية ١٣١.١٢ آية ١٣٥.

**English**:
> Actually: in the Takwīr ﴿And you do not will except that Allāh wills - Sovereign Sustainer and Evolver of all realms of existence.﴾ 1 say ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) Maryam ﵍ M: five actually: place M: ﴿And warn them, O Messenger, of the Day of Regret, when the matter will be concluded; and yet, they are in a state of heedlessness, and they do not believe.﴾ 2, ﴿So be not impatient over them. We only count out to them a limited number.﴾ 3, ﴿Say, "Whoever is in error - let the Most Merciful extend for him an extension in wealth and time until, when they see that which they were promised - either punishment in this world or the Hour of resurrection - they will come to know who is worst in position and weaker in soldiers."﴾ 4 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿But there came after them successors who neglected the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) and pursued desires; so they are going to meet evil -﴾ 5 actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by it (baʿdahu) and he ﴿Except those who repent, believe and do righteousness; for those will enter Paradise and will not be wronged at all.﴾ 6 M: ﴿And there is none of you except he will come to it. This is upon your Sovereign Lord an inevitability decreed.﴾ 7 actually: ﴿Then We will save those who feared Allāh and leave the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn) within it, on their knees.﴾ 8 Sūrah (Chapter of) Ṭā-Hā M: four actually: place M: ﴿So be patient over what they say and exalt Allāh with praise of your Sovereign Lord before the rising of the sun and before its setting; and during periods of the night exalt Him and at the ends of the day, that you may be satisfied.﴾ 10, ﴿And do not extend your eyes toward that by which We have given enjoyment to some categories of them, its being but the splendor of worldly life by which We test them. And the provision of your Sovereign Lord is better and more enduring.﴾ 11, ﴿Say, "Each of us is waiting; so wait. For you will know who are the companions of the sound path and who is guided."﴾ 12 1 the Takwīr 29. 2 a verse (āyah) 39. 3 a verse (āyah) 84. 4 a verse (āyah) 75. 5 a verse (āyah) 59. 6 a verse (āyah) 60. 7 a verse (āyah) 71. 8 a verse (āyah) 72. 9 in the foundational principle: be patient, persevere. and the correct (ṣawāb) what in the Qur'ānic Codex (al-Muṣḥaf) the exalted in. 10 a verse (āyah) 130. 11 a verse (āyah) 131. 12 a verse (āyah) 135.

And the provision of your Sovereign Lord is better and more enduring.﴾ 11, ﴿Say, "Each of us is waiting; so wait. **English**:
> Actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M and not seek to hasten, demand quickly by from before that decree, judge to me (ilayya) you his Prophetic Revelation (Waḥy) 1 actually: ﴿We will make you recite, O Messenger, and you will not forget,﴾ 2 Sūrah (Chapter of) the Prophets ﵈ M: three actually: three M: ﴿Indeed, you disbelievers and what you worship other than Allāh are the firewood of the Hellfire of retributive consequence (Jahannam). You will be coming to enter it.﴾ 3 the Verses (al-Āyāt) the three actually: its Laʿmūm ﴿Indeed, those for whom the best reward has preceded from Us - they are from it far removed.﴾ 4 the Verses (al-Āyāt) the three Sūrah (Chapter of) the Pilgrimage (al-Ḥajj) M: four actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: say O O mankind Truly I Lakm warner clear, manifest, making clear 5 actually: its judgment not for the Faẓh a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿And if they dispute with you, then say, "Allāh is ever most knowing of what you do.﴾ 6 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿That has been commanded, and whoever honors the sacred ordinances of Allāh - it is best for him in the sight of his Sovereign Lord. And permitted to you are the grazing livestock, except what is recited to you. So avoid the uncleanliness of idols and avoid false statement,﴾ 7 actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by them (baʿdahā) and he ﴿O you who have attained faith! Fulfill your contractual covenants and solemn obligations in their entirety...﴾ 8 M: ﴿And strive for Allāh with the striving due to Him. He has chosen you and has not placed upon you in the religion any difficulty. It is the religion of your father, Abraham. Allāh named you "Muslims" before in former scriptures and in this revelation that the Messenger may be a witness over you may be witnesses over the people. So establish the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) and give the purifying equity of Zakāh and hold fast to Allāh. He is your protector; and excellent is the protector, and excellent is the helper.﴾ 9 1 a verse (āyah) 114. 2 high 6. 3 the Verses (al-Āyāt) 98 - 100. 4 the Verses (al-Āyāt) 101 - 103. 5 a verse (āyah) 49. 6 a verse (āyah) 68. 7 a verse (āyah) 30. 8 a verse (āyah) 30. 9 a verse (āyah) 78.

### Folio 24 (`Page ID: 2209-24` | `149` Words | `12` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> "ن": في التغابن ﴿فَاتَّقُوا اللَّهَ مَا اسْتَطَعْتُمْ﴾ ١ سورة المؤمنين "م": موضعان"ن": موضع"م": ﴿فَذَرْهُمْ فِي غَمْرَتِهِمْ حَتَّى حِينٍ﴾ ٢، ﴿ادْفَعْ بِالَّتِي هِيَ أَحْسَنُ السَّيِّئَةَ﴾ ٣"ن": آية السيف سورة النور "م": ثمانية"ن": عشرة"م": ﴿فَإِنْ تَوَلَّوْا فَإِنَّمَا عَلَيْهِ مَا حُمِّلَ وَعَلَيْكُمْ مَا حُمِّلْتُمْ﴾ ٤"ن": آية السيف"م": ﴿وَلا تَقْبَلُوا "٩٣ ب" لَهُمْ شَهَادَةً أَبَداً وَأُولَئِكَ هُمُ الْفَاسِقُونَ﴾ ٥"ن": الاستثناء بعده وهو ﴿إِلَّا الَّذِينَ تَابُوا﴾ ٦"م": ﴿الزَّانِي لا يَنْكِحُ إِلَّا زَانِيَةً أَوْ مُشْرِكَةً﴾ ٧ خبر معناه النهي"ن": لحكم المشركين ﴿وَأَنْكِحُوا الْأَيَامَى مِنْكُمْ﴾ ٨"م": ﴿وَالَّذِينَ يَرْمُونَ الْمُحْصَنَاتِ﴾ ٩"ن": لبعض حكمها ﴿وَالَّذِينَ يَرْمُونَ أَزْوَاجَهُمْ إلى الصَّادِقِينَ﴾ ١٠"م": ﴿يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا لا تَدْخُلُوا بُيُوتاً غَيْرَ بُيُوتِكُمْ حَتَّى تَسْتَأْنِسُوا﴾ ١١
١ التغابن ١٦.٢ آية ٥٤.٣ آية ٩٦.٤ آية ٥٤.٥ آية ٤.٦ آية ٥.٧ آية ٣.٨ آية ٣١.٩ آية ٤.١٠ آية ٦.١١ آية ٢٧.

**English**:
> Actually: in the Taghābn ﴿So fear Allāh as much as you are able and listen and obey and spend in the way of Allāh; it is better for your selves. And whoever is protected from the stinginess of his soul - it is those who will be the successful.﴾ 1 Sūrah (Chapter of) believer M: place actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿So leave them in their confusion for a time.﴾ 2, ﴿Repel, by means of what is best, their evil. We are most knowing of what they describe.﴾ 3 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) the light M: eight actually: ten M: ﴿Say, "Obey Allāh and obey the Messenger; but if you turn away - then upon him is only that duty with which he has been charged, and upon you is that with which you have been charged. And if you obey him, you will be rightly guided. And there is not upon the Messenger except the responsibility for clear notification."﴾ 4 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: and not suffice 93 B to a testimony (shahādah) forever those they defiantly disobedient 5 actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by it (baʿdahu) and he ﴿Except for those who repent and correct themselves and make evident what they concealed. Those - I will accept their repentance, and I am the Accepting of repentance, the Merciful.﴾ 6 M: ﴿The fornicator does not marry except a female fornicator or polytheist, and none marries her except a fornicator or a polytheist, and that has been made unlawful to those who have attained firm conviction in truth (Al-Mu'minūn).﴾ 7 predicate (khabar) its meaning is the Prohibitive Command (al-Nahy) actually: judgment one who associates partners ﴿And marry the unmarried among you and the upright in dynamic virtue and equilibrium (Al-Abrār) among your male slaves and female slaves. If they should be poor, Allāh shall enrich them from His bounty, and Allāh is ever all-Encompassing and Knowing.﴾ 8 M: ﴿And those who accuse chaste women and then do not produce four witnesses - lash them with eighty lashes and do not accept from them testimony ever after. And those are the defiantly disobedient,﴾ 9 actually: some, part of the legal ruling on her ﴿And those who accuse their wives of adultery and have no witnesses except themselves - then the witness of one of them shall be four testimonies swearing by Allāh that indeed, he is of the truthful.﴾ 10 M: O O those who believed not enter house other than your house until humanity 11 1 the Taghābn 16. 2 a verse (āyah) 54. 3 a verse (āyah) 96. 4 a verse (āyah) 54. 5 a verse (āyah) 4. 6 a verse (āyah) 5. 7 a verse (āyah) 3. 8 a verse (āyah) 31. 9 a verse (āyah) 4. 10 a verse (āyah) 6. 11 a verse (āyah) 27.

**English**:
> Actually: some, part of the legal ruling on her ﴿There is no blame upon you for entering houses not inhabited in which there is convenience for you. And Allāh knows what you reveal and what you conceal.﴾ 1 M: ﴿And tell the believing women to reduce some of their vision and guard their private parts and not expose their adornment except that which necessarily appears thereof and to wrap a portion of their headcovers over their chests and not expose their adornment except to their husbands, their fathers, their husbands' fathers, their sons, their husbands' sons, their brothers, their brothers' sons, their sisters' sons, their women, that which their right hands possess, or those male attendants having no physical desire, or children who are not yet aware of the private aspects of women. And let them not stamp their feet to make known what they conceal of their adornment. And turn to Allāh in repentance, all of you, O believers, that you might succeed.﴾ 2 actually some, part of the legal ruling on her ﴿And women of post-menstrual age who have no desire for marriage - there is no blame upon them for putting aside their outer garments but not displaying adornment. But to modestly refrain from that is better for them. And Allāh is ever Hearing and Knowing.﴾ then abrogated also (ayḍan) by his statement (bi-qawlihi) (Exalted is He): ﴿And women of post-menstrual age who have no desire for marriage - there is no blame upon them for putting aside their outer garments but not displaying adornment. But to modestly refrain from that is better for them. And Allāh is ever Hearing and Knowing.﴾ 4 M O O those who believed is not you those who possess, own, have power over faith you 5 actually: ﴿And when the children among you reach puberty, let them ask permission at all times as those before them have done. Thus does Allāh make clear to you His verses; and Allāh is ever Knowing and Wise.﴾ 6 Sūrah (Chapter of) al-Furqān (The Criterion) M: four actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿And the servants of the Most Merciful are those who walk upon the terrestrial earth easily, and when the ignorant address them harshly, they say words of peace,﴾ 7 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿And those who do not invoke with Allāh another deity or kill the soul which Allāh has forbidden to be killed, except by right, and do not commit unlawful sexual intercourse. And whoever should do that will meet a penalty.﴾ 8 the two Verses (al-Āyatān) actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by them both (baʿdahumā) and he ﴿Except those who repent, believe and do righteousness; for those will enter Paradise and will not be wronged at all.﴾ 9 Sūrah (Chapter of) the poets (al-Shuʿarāʾ) M: three actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: poet follow them the consciousness 10 the Verses (al-Āyāt) the three actually: exception (al-Istithnāʾ) followed by them (baʿdahā) and he ﴿Except those poets who believe and do righteous deeds and remember Allāh often and defend the Muslims after they were wronged. **English**:
> Upon Allah 1, ﴿And whoever is patient and forgives - indeed, that is of the matters requiring determination.﴾ 2, ﴿But if they turn away - then We have not sent you, O Messenger, over them as a guardian; upon you is only the duty of notification. And indeed, when We let man taste mercy from us, he rejoices in it; but if evil afflicts him for what his hands have put forth, then indeed, man is ungrateful.﴾ 3 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: ﴿So to that religion of Allāh invite, O Messenger, and remain on a right course as you are commanded and do not follow their inclinations but say, "I have believed in what Allāh has revealed of the Qur'an, and I have been commanded to do justice among you. Allāh is ever our Sovereign Lord and your Sovereign Lord. For us are our deeds, and for you your deeds. There is no need for argument between us and you. Allāh shall bring us together, and to Him is the final destination."﴾ 4 actually: a verse (āyah) fighting, battle M: ﴿the heavens almost break from above them, and the celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah) exalt Allāh with praise of their Sovereign Lord and ask forgiveness for those on earth. Unquestionably, it is Allāh who is the Oft-Forgiving, the Dispenser of Sustaining Grace.﴾ 5 actually: general her in forgive ﴿Those celestial angels (Al-Malā'ikah) (Malā'ikah) who carry the Throne and those around it exalt Allāh with praise of their Sovereign Lord and believe in Him and ask forgiveness for those who have believed, saying, "our Sovereign Lord, You have encompassed all things in mercy and knowledge, so forgive those who have repented and followed Your way and protect them from the punishment of Hellfire.﴾ 6 M: ﴿Whoever desires the harvest of the Hereafter - We increase for him in his harvest. And whoever desires the harvest of this world - We give him thereof, but there is not for him in the Hereafter any share.﴾ 7 actually: in Glory be to ﴿Whoever should desire the immediate - We hasten for him from it what We will to whom We intend. **English**:
> M: ﴿And whether or not We take you away in death, indeed, We will take retribution upon them.﴾ 1, ﴿So turn aside from them and say, "Peace." But they are going to know.﴾ 2, ﴿So leave them to converse vainly and amuse themselves until they meet their Day which they are promised.﴾ 3 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) the Dakhān M: place M: ﴿Then watch for the Day when the sky will bring a visible smoke.﴾ 4, ﴿So watch, O Messenger; indeed, they are watching for your end.﴾ 5. actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) the knees M: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿Say, O Messenger, to those who have believed that they should forgive those who expect not the days of Allāh so that He may recompense a people for what they used to earn.﴾ 6. actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) the Aḥqāf M: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿Say, "I am not something original among the messengers, nor do I know what will be done with me or with you. I only follow that which is revealed to me, and I am not but a clear warner."﴾ 7. actually: ﴿That Allāh may forgive for you what preceded of your sin and what will follow and complete His favor upon you and guide you to a Straight Path﴾ 8. Sūrah (Chapter of) fighting, battle 9 M: place. actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿So when you meet those who stubbornly reject established reality (Al-Kāfirūn) in battle, strike their necks until, when you have inflicted slaughter upon them, then secure their bonds, and either confer favor afterwards or ransom them until the war lays down its burdens. That is the command. And if Allāh had willed, He could have taken vengeance upon them Himself, but He ordered armed struggle to test some of you by means of others. And those who are killed in the cause of Allāh - never will He waste their deeds.﴾ 10. 1 a verse (āyah) 41. 2 a verse (āyah) 89. 3 a verse (āyah) 83. 4 a verse (āyah) 10. 5 a verse (āyah) 59. 6 a verse (āyah) 14. 7 a verse (āyah) 9. 8 al-Fatḥ 2. 9 she Sūrah (Chapter of) Muḥammad in the Qur'ānic Codex (al-Muṣḥaf) the exalted in. 10 a verse (āyah) 4.

**English**:
> M: ﴿And what Allāh restored to His Messenger from the people of the towns - it is for Allāh and for the Messenger and for his near relatives and orphans and the stranded traveler - so that it will not be a perpetual distribution among the rich from among you. And whatever the Messenger has given you - take; and what he has forbidden you - refrain from. And fear Allāh; indeed, Allāh is ever severe in penalty.﴾... to His statement and Ibn al-Sabīl 1 actually: in addition ﴿And know that anything you obtain of war booty - then indeed, for Allāh is ever one fifth of it and for the Messenger and for his near relatives and the orphans, the needy, and the stranded traveler, if you have believed in Allāh and in that which We sent down to Our Servant on the day of criterion - the day when the two armies met. And Allāh, over all things, is competent.﴾ 2 Sūrah (Chapter of) the Mumtaḥinah M: three actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿Allāh does not forbid you from those who do not fight you because of religion and do not expel you from your homes - from being righteous toward them and acting justly toward them. Indeed, Allāh loves those who act justly.﴾ 3 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: not forbid you Allah from those who did not your Yqātlū in the debt Religion (al-dayn-dīn) 4 actually: Truly forbid you Allah from those who your Qātlū in the debt Religion (al-dayn-dīn) 5 M: and spear what spend 6 actually: in free of blame ﴿This is a declaration of disassociation, from Allāh and His Messenger, to those with whom you had made a treaty among those who associate partners with Allāh (Al-Mushrikūn).﴾ 7 Sūrah (Chapter of) the typesetting and Sūrah Friday (al-Jumuʿah) ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) hypocrites ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) the Taghābn ruling. 1 a verse (āyah) 7. 2 addition 4. 3 a verse (āyah) 8. 4 a verse (āyah) 8. 5 a verse (āyah) 9. 6 a verse (āyah) 10. 7 repentance (al-Tawbah) 1.

**Arabic**:
> "ن": في براءة ﴿خُذْ مِن: أَمْوَالِهِمْ صَدَقَةً﴾ ١، ﴿إِنمَا الصَّدَقَاتُ لِلْفُقَرَاءِ﴾ ٢ سورة نوح ﵇ وسورة الجن ٣محكمتان سورة المزمل "م": تسعة"ن": موضعان"م": ﴿وَاصْبِرْ عَلَى مَا يَقُولُونَ وَاهْجُرْهُمْ هَجْراً جَمِيلا﴾ ٤، ﴿وَذَرْنِي وَالْمُكَذِّبِينَ﴾ ٥، ﴿إِنَّ هَذِهِ تَذْكِرَةٌ فَمَنْ شَاءَ اتَّخَذَ إِلَى رَبِّهِ سَبِيلاً﴾ ٦"ن": آية السيف"م": ﴿قُمِ اللَّيْلَ إِلَّا قَلِيلاً ... إلى قِيلاً﴾ ٧ الآيات الخمس٨"ن": ﴿إِنَّ رَبَّكَ يَعْلَمُ أَنَّكَ تَقُومُ أَدْنَى مِنْ ثُلُثَيِ اللَّيْلِ﴾ ٩ الآية سورة المدثر "م": ثلاثة١٠"ن": موضعان"م": ﴿ذَرْنِي وَمَنْ خَلَقْتُ وَحِيداً﴾ ١١"ن": آية السيف.
١ التوبة ١٠٣.٢ التوبة ٦٠.٣ في الأصل: فاصبر. وما أثبتناه من المصحف الشريف.٤ آية ١٠.٥ آية ١١.٦ آية ١٩.٧ الآيات ٢ - ٦.٨ في الأصل: الثلاث.٩ الإنسان.١٠ في الأصل: موضعان.١١ آية ١١.

**English**:
> Actually: in free of blame ﴿Take, O, Muhammad, from their wealth a charity by which you purify them and cause them increase, and invoke Allāh 's blessings upon them. Indeed, your invocations are reassurance for them. And Allāh is ever Hearing and Knowing.﴾ 1, ﴿the purifying equity of Zakāh expenditures are only for the poor and for the needy and for those employed to collect the purifying equity of Zakāh and for bringing hearts together for Islam and for freeing captives or slaves and for those in debt and for the cause of Allāh and for the stranded traveler - an obligation imposed by Allāh. And Allāh is ever Knowing and Wise.﴾ 2 Sūrah (Chapter of) Nūḥ ﵇ and Sūrah jinn 3 ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) the Muzmil M: nine actually: place M: ﴿And be patient over what they say and avoid them with gracious avoidance.﴾ 4, leave, let be me deniers 5, ﴿Indeed, this is a reminder, so whoever wills may take to his Sovereign Lord a way.﴾ 6 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) M: stand the night except little, few... to acknowledges 7 the Verses (al-Āyāt) the five actually: ﴿Indeed, your Sovereign Lord knows, O Messenger, that you stand in the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) almost two thirds of the night or half of it or a third of it, and so do a group of those with you. And Allāh determines the extent of the night and the day. He has known that you Muslims will not be able to do it and has turned to you in forgiveness, so recite what is easy for you of the Qur'an. He has known that there will be among you those who are ill and others traveling throughout the land seeking something of the bounty of Allāh and others fighting for the cause of Allāh. So recite what is easy from it and establish the regular prayer (Al-Ṣalāh) and give the purifying equity of Zakāh and loan Allāh a goodly loan. And whatever good you put forward for yourselves - you will find it with Allāh. It is better and greater in reward. And seek forgiveness of Allāh. Indeed, Allāh is ever Forgiving and Merciful.﴾ 9 Verse (al-Āyah) Sūrah (Chapter of) the Mudthir M: three actually: place M: ﴿Leave Me with the one I created alone﴾ 11 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf). 1 repentance (al-Tawbah) 103. 2 repentance (al-Tawbah) 60. 3 in the foundational principle: be patient, persevere. and what his Athbatn from the Qur'ānic Codex (al-Muṣḥaf) the exalted in. 4 a verse (āyah) 10. 5 a verse (āyah) 11. 6 a verse (āyah) 19. 7 the Verses (al-Āyāt) 2 - 6. 8 in the foundational principle: the three. 9 the human being (al-Insān). 10 in the foundational principle: place. 11 a verse (āyah) 11.

> سورة عبس "م": موضع"م": ﴿فَمَنْ شَاءَ ذَكَرَهُ﴾ ١"ن": ﴿وَمَا تَشَاءُونَ إِلَّا أَنْ يَشَاءَ اللَّهُ﴾ ٢ سورة التكوير "م": موضع"ن": موضع"م": ﴿لِمَنْ شَاءَ مِنْكُمْ أَنْ يَسْتَقِيمَ﴾ ٣"ن": ﴿وَمَا تَشَاءُونَ إِلَّا أَنْ يَشَاءَ اللَّهُ﴾ ٤وقيل محكمة سورة الانفطار والمطففين والانشقاق والبروج محكمات سورة طارق "م": موضع"م": ﴿فَمَهِّلِ الْكَافِرِينَ أَمْهِلْهُمْ رُوَيْداً﴾ ٥"ن": آية السيف سورة الأعلى محكمة.
١ آية ١١. وفي الأصل ﴿فمن شاء فليؤمن ومن شاء فليكفر﴾ .وهو سهو ينظر. ابن حزم ١٣٦، ابن سلامة ٩٨، العتائقي ٨٢ ابن المتوج ٢٠٥.٢ الإنسان ٣٠.٣ آية ٢٨.٤ آية ٣٩.٥ آية ١٧.

**English**:
> Sūrah (Chapter of) ʿAbs M: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿Then whoever wills will remember it.﴾ 1 actually: ﴿And you do not will except that Allāh wills. Indeed, Allāh is ever Knowing and Wise.﴾ 2 Sūrah (Chapter of) the Takwīr M: place locus (mawḍiʿ) actually: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿For whoever wills among you to take a right course.﴾ 3 actually: ﴿And you do not will except that Allāh wills. Indeed, Allāh is ever Knowing and Wise.﴾ 4 say ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) the Originated with Inherent Order and the Muṭfif and the split and the stars ruling Sūrah (Chapter of) transmission path M: place locus (mawḍiʿ) M: ﴿So allow time for the rejecters of established truth (Al-Kāfirūn). Leave them awhile.﴾ 5 actually: a verse (āyah) the sword (al-Sayf) Sūrah (Chapter of) high ruling. 1 a verse (āyah) 11. And in the foundational principle ﴿And say, "The truth is from your Sovereign Lord, so whoever wills - let him believe; and whoever wills - let him disbelieve." Indeed, We have prepared for the wrongdoers and violators of balance (Al-Ẓālimūn) a fire whose walls will surround them. And if they call for relief, they will be relieved with water like murky oil, which scalds their faces. Wretched is the drink, and evil is the resting place.﴾ and he Sahū investigates. Ibn Ḥazm 136, Ibn Salāmah 98, the insolent 82 Ibn al-Mutwij 205. 2 the human being (al-Insān) 30. 3 a verse (āyah) 28. 4 a verse (āyah) 39. 5 a verse (āyah) 17.
